Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 7108 after decompression) · Source record

A machine leg extension that isolates the front of the thigh. It builds quad size and strength.

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 7113 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: supported_lying.

A pullover variation with straight arms that stretches and works the chest and lats through a long arc.

Proposed staging

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Lie on a bench holding one dumbbell above your chest with straight arms. 7113.json#/instructions/0
  2. Keep a slight softness in your elbows and brace your core. 7113.json#/instructions/1
  3. Lower the weight in an arc back over your head until you feel a stretch. 7113.json#/instructions/2
  4. Keep your ribs down and your hips on the bench. 7113.json#/instructions/3
  5. Pull the dumbbell back over your chest. 7113.json#/instructions/4
  6. Repeat with a smooth tempo. 7113.json#/instructions/5

7154 — side shuffle high knee

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 7154 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A lateral shuffle where each step brings the knee high. It raises your heart rate and builds agility.

Proposed staging
